  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (466)`** (pushed to **472** by the audit round below - extra A1 / A6 guards) — the source-anchor block is re-anchored and gains `function parallaxPluginEffectiveGroups()` / `const plugins = parallaxPluginEffectiveGroups();` / `if (st.ui && typeof document !== 'undefined' && document` / `if (PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT > max) max = PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT;` / `Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(st.plugin, rec.slot)` / `const sign = raw < 0 ? -1 : 1;` / `if (prev && (prev > 0) !== (raw > 0)) return 0;` / `if (!prev) {`; the fake-DOM harness gains a second plugin outlet (`data-slot="otheracc.widget"`) whose element child **lives inside the native conversation group's box**, and three legs pin it down: with interface follow **off** it moves and the list holds both it and `dshmarket.panel`; with interface follow **on** it must **not** get a single `translate` of its own (absorbed by the outer group) and must **not** appear in the list while `dshmarket.panel` still does; the keyless-default leg = wallpaper distance 0 + interface follow off + plugin on ⇒ jumping from zero to the full step must move only part of the way on the **first** frame (strictly less than the settled value); the zero-crossing leg samples the conversation text area's displacement frame by frame and asserts that both a negative and a positive value occur and that no two adjacent frames have opposite signs. The harness's `pointermove` / `selection` timing was fixed along the way too (this layer "settles and stops", and a rescan only happens inside a kick ⇒ after changing a switch the fake clock must first be pushed past the 250ms rescan throttle and the pointer moved to a **new** position, or the new record is never created — see the new `rescan()` helper's comment; those legs used to be "green without having run"). The island block gains `PARALLAX_GROUP_DEPTH_MIN` / `_MAX` stand-ins (when imported alone they are sibling symbols in the same build-time scope — read live from the layer source as `const NAME = N;`, **no number copied by hand**) and an out-of-range readout leg (`{ [SLOT_A]: 42, [SLOT_B]: -5 }` ⇒ the readout lands on the two in-range endpoints). `test/verify-client.mjs` walks the tree and pins both "the plugin switch is off by default" and "with it off that card shows only the switch". `lib/client.js` is rebuilt in the same commit. **A client-only change ⇒ a page refresh is enough.**

- **"3D depth": the four native front-end region distances now ship at the values the user tuned (1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4)** (user request: "modify the defaults for the native front end when it is enabled", together with those four numbers; the "Interface elements follow" switch itself stays off by default).
  **What**: the four `DEFAULTS` lines in `lib/settings-schema.js` change from `1` each to `parallaxUiChatDepth: 1.2` / `parallaxUiComposerDepth: 1.8` / `parallaxUiSidebarDepth: 1.6` / `parallaxUiBubbleDepth: 1.4` (**KINDS unchanged** — the slider domain is still `num 0..10` with step 0.1, only the default moves); the layer's four "fallback when the setting cannot be read" constants (`PARALLAX_GROUP_CHAT / COMPOSER / SIDEBAR / BUBBLE`) move to `1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4` in step, literally equal to `DEFAULTS`; the "Composer travel" tooltip in `src/ext-parallax.js` drops the hard-coded "(1% by default)" for "by default a little larger than the text area", with `src/i18n-copy.js` updated on both sides (panel copy no longer repeats any default value — `lib/settings-schema.js` stays the only place to look one up).
  **Why**: the shipping set used to be the conservative "1% each", and the user measured and gave the layering he actually wants (composer nearest at 1.8, sidebar 1.6, user bubble 1.4, conversation text area 1.2) — the first time "Interface elements follow" is switched on it should already look tuned instead of making everyone drag four sliders first. **Distances only, not the switch**: `parallaxUi` stays off by default (an effect should not change what everyone sees out of the box, the same reason as elsewhere on this page). The plugin group's default `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT` was deliberately **not** moved along with it (it is its own number, still 1%, still the same order of magnitude as the native regions).
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (466)`** (pushed to **472** by the audit round below - extra A1 / A6 guards) — the four constant anchors become `const PARALLAX_GROUP_CHAT = 1.2;` / `const PARALLAX_GROUP_COMPOSER = 1.8;` / `const PARALLAX_GROUP_SIDEBAR = 1.6;` / `const PARALLAX_GROUP_BUBBLE = 1.4;` (the behaviour harness still passes its own four explicit values, so the per-region and "set to 0" legs are unaffected). `test/verify-client.mjs` replaces the four "always `1%`" readout assertions with a per-row table `{ '会话文本区距离': '1.2%', '输入卡片距离': '1.8%', '侧栏距离': '1.6%', '用户气泡距离': '1.4%' }` (the 0..10 domain and "no rows while the interface switch is off" stay pinned). `test/verify-i18n.mjs` passes all 41 checks (the tooltip's zh key and en value change together). `test/fixtures/settings-sanitize-golden.json` is re-recorded against the new `DEFAULTS` (33 value changes across the 18 cases x client / host sides, **zero drift on every other key**; script `.test-cache/regen-golden-native-defaults.mjs`). `lib/client.js` is rebuilt in the same commit. **A client-only change ⇒ a page refresh is enough**, no host restart. **Migration note (audit #5)**: what changed here is a **default** — it only applies to profiles where those four keys are not in the stored settings yet; a profile that has stored them (any profile that has saved settings at least once, since `serializeSettings()` writes the whole KINDS table) keeps the values it has, so the new shipping set has to be dragged in by hand there. `settingsVersion` does **not** move, there is **no** migration script and **no** unit changed.

- **"3D depth": the plugin front end gets its own switch (off by default) — each of the three front-end classes now has its own switch, and "Interface elements follow" no longer decides for it** (user wording: "give the plugin front end its own category and switch"; ruling = **a separate switch, off by default**).
  **What**: a new setting key `parallaxPlugin` (one line each in `lib/settings-schema.js` DEFAULTS and KINDS, `boolFalse`, shipping `false`). ① **The layer** (`src/parallax-layer.js`): `parallaxSettings()` gains `pluginOn: selection.parallaxPlugin === true,`; `parallaxMaxPercent(st)` is reordered into "take the wallpaper first, then the largest of the four regions while `st.ui`, and only past `if (!st.pluginOn) return max;` walk the plugin table" (the old early return that hung the whole plugin table on `st.ui` is gone); the plugin branch of `parallaxTargetRatio()` becomes `if (rec.kind === 'plugin') { if (!st.pluginOn) return 0; … }` and moves **ahead of** `if (!st.ui) return 0;` ⇒ the plugin groups no longer eat the interface switch; the block in `parallaxTargetsRefresh()` that recognises plugin groups becomes `if (parallaxSettings().pluginOn) {` — while it is off they are **not even scanned** (rather than "coefficient computed as 0"), because scanning would only waste a subtree check for `position: fixed` descendants (up to 400 `getComputedStyle` calls per group) on a result that is certainly 0. ② **The panel** (`src/ext-parallax.js`): the first row of the "Plugin front end" card becomes its own switch, `switchRow(weT("插件前端跟随"), sel.parallaxPlugin === true, onParallaxPlugin, …)`, and its hint, both hint states (slots found / none found) and the per-slot `SliderRow`s all move from `ui &&` to `pluginOn &&` ⇒ with the switch off that card shows **only the switch** (the same invariant as "you can still drag values while it is off"), and the slot list and "what actually moves" now share one source. ③ **Host-side wiring** (`src/client.js`): a new named handler `onParallaxPlugin` (`setSetting("parallaxPlugin", e.target.checked)`) is wired into `extensionCtx()`, and `parallaxPluginSlots` now tests `sel.parallaxPlugin === true` instead of `sel.parallaxUi === true`; the comment on the `parallaxPluginDepths` table in `lib/settings-schema.js` gains "it only counts while the switch is on (the table keeps its values and they apply again the moment it is switched back on)". ④ Two copy entries (`src/i18n-copy.js`: "插件前端跟随" and its English hint), and the file header plus the doc above `parallaxTargetRatio()` now say that each of the three classes has its own switch.
  **Why**: the plugin front end moves **the real interface drawn by other plugins** (a task board, a marketplace panel, …), which is a different matter from the native front end — a user may want the host interface they look at all day to drift while third-party panels stay put (or the other way round). **Separate** (rather than a sub-switch under "Interface elements follow") is the only shape in which all four combinations exist; **off by default** follows the same reason as `parallaxUi` (an effect should not change what everyone sees out of the box). **Rejected**: making it a sub-switch of `parallaxUi` — that ties the two decisions together, and with `parallaxUi` off the plugin switch would disappear, i.e. there would be no switch at all. **Kept**: the table is preserved while the switch is off (the same convention as the four region distances under `parallaxUi`).
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (466)`** (pushed to **472** by the audit round below - extra A1 / A6 guards) — four new source anchors (`pluginOn: selection.parallaxPlugin === true,` / `if (parallaxSettings().pluginOn) {` / `if (!st.pluginOn) return 0;` / `if (!st.pluginOn) return max;`) plus two negative anchors (the source must **no longer** contain `if (!st.ui) return max;` or `parallaxSettings().ui`); the fake-DOM harness gains a "plugin slot outlet (`display: contents`, `data-slot="dshmarket.panel"`) with a boxed element child" and three legs — interface follow on while the plugin switch is off ⇒ the slot does not move at all; the plugin switch alone on ⇒ that group travels exactly as far as the native conversation group (1% by default, compared literally on both axes); turning the whole interface block off then leaves the plugin group moving while none of the native groups move; the closing "everything is cleaned up" check now also covers the plugin outlet and its child. The island gains an `onPlugin` case (`PARAMS` / `UI_PARAMS` each gain a "Plugin interface follows" row, the old `onSlots` is renamed `onUiSlots` and tightened to "interface on + plugin off ⇒ not a single plugin row may appear", and `onPlugin` expects the order background / mascot / interface / plugin switch + the two slot keys + easing smoothness), plus a source-anchor check for the plugin switch (`const pluginOn = on && sel.parallaxPlugin === true;` / `const slots = pluginOn && Array.isArray(parallaxPluginSlots) ? … : [];` / `switchRow(weT("插件前端跟随")…` / `pluginOn && (slots.length` / `pluginOn && slots.map(` with the negatives `ui && (slots.length` / `ui && slots.map(`, and `function onParallaxPlugin(e) {…}`, `parallaxPluginSlots: sel.parallaxPlugin === true ? …`, schema `DEFAULTS.parallaxPlugin === false` and `KINDS.parallaxPlugin.kind === 'boolFalse'`). `test/verify-client.mjs` asserts by walking the tree that the plugin switch is off by default, that **with it off that card has neither a hint nor a slot row**, that "no other plugin's front-end element groups found yet" only appears once it is switched on (that fixture has no DOM, so this is the only state it can see), and that turning "Interface elements follow" off leaves the plugin card untouched (switch still on, hint still there) while the four native rows collapse — the two switches are independent. `test/verify-i18n.mjs` green across all 41 checks. `test/fixtures/settings-sanitize-golden.json` re-recorded from the code (33 new `"parallaxPlugin": false` entries across both sides of the 18 cases, **zero drift on every other key**; the re-record script is `.test-cache/regen-golden-plugin.mjs`). `lib/client.js` rebuilt (in the same commit as `src/**`).

- **"3D depth" easing distances now share one meaning — "the largest displacement as a share of the screen's longest diagonal" — the layer auto-discovers front-end element groups registered by other plugins, and the panel splits into three cards: Background / Native front end / Plugin front end** (user report, three points: ① "note that the percentage here means 'the largest easing distance as a share of the screen diagonal length'"; ② "next, you could try auto-detecting front-end element groups registered by other plugins and let them ease too"; ③ "split the easing element settings under Extensions into categories: background, native front end, plugin front end, and when an element's maximum easing distance is set to 0 that element does not ease"; two further rulings: slider domain `0–10%`, step `0.1%`, shipping at `1%` for every row, and plugin element groups **participate by default** — ⚠️ both are superseded by the entries above: every row now ships at 1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4 (user request m04159), and the plugin front end now has **its own switch** (`parallaxPlugin`), **off by default**, so "participate by default" no longer holds).
  **What**: ① **One meaning for a percentage**: `src/parallax-layer.js` gains `const PARALLAX_STEP_DIV = 50;` and the frame becomes `targetX = PARALLAX_DIRECTION * (cx - vw / 2) / PARALLAX_STEP_DIV` (same for Y) — i.e. `displacement = 2 x pct/100 x (cursor - screen center)`, so with the cursor in a screen corner (`|u| = diagonal/2`) `|displacement|` is exactly `pct%` x diagonal; the wallpaper overscan in `src/styles.js` moves in step, from `scale: calc(1 + multiplier / 100)` to `calc(1 + multiplier / 50)` (enlarged by `1 + pct/50`). ② **The four region keys store an absolute percentage and the total factor retires**: `parallaxUiChatDepth` / `parallaxUiComposerDepth` / `parallaxUiSidebarDepth` / `parallaxUiBubbleDepth` go from KINDS `num 0..3` to `num 0..10` with DEFAULTS `1` each, while `parallaxUiDepth` (the interface-follow **total factor**) is removed entirely — its KINDS entry, its slider row in `src/ext-parallax.js` and its `onParallaxUiDepth` handler in `src/client.js` all go (a key that is not in KINDS is simply dropped by `sanitizeFromSchema()` as it walks the table ⇒ orphan keys in old saved settings need no migration and `settingsVersion` does not move); the layer's four `PARALLAX_GROUP_*` coefficients (1 / 1.5 / 0.6 / 0.4, only "the fallback when the setting cannot be read") all become `1` and `PARALLAX_GROUP_DEPTH_MAX` goes 3 → 10; the four slider rows' `Math.round(sel.parallaxUi*Depth * 100)` and the four handlers' `v / 100` are deleted, so the panel and the stored value finally **share one unit** (the `%` suffix is a unit, not a x100 display layer). ③ **Plugin front end**: a new setting key `parallaxPluginDepths` (KINDS `map` — a shallow copy, values unvalidated, the same shape as `frameVariants`; semantics = slot key -> percentage); the layer gains `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_SLOT_ATTR / _SELECTOR / _SKIP / _SKIP_PREFIX / _SKIP_SCOPE` and `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT = 1`, plus `parallaxPluginKey(el)` (reads `data-slot`, filters by the skip list and prefixes, drops the settings and plugin-management subtrees via `el.closest(PARALLAX_PLUGIN_SKIP_SCOPE)`), `parallaxPluginGroups()` (returns `[{ el: the outlet's element child, slot }]` — the outlet itself is `display: contents` and has no box, see the previous "3D depth" entry and `DSH-UI-INTERFACES.md` §3.5) and `parallaxDiscoveredGroups()` (the de-duplicated slot-key array); those candidates are enqueued through `parallaxTargetAdd(…, 'plugin', slot)` and their coefficient comes from `st.plugin[rec.slot]` (a missing key means `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT`, an explicit 0 is kept and means that group does not move); the frame's "largest percentage" now comes from the new `parallaxMaxPercent(st)` rather than the wallpaper alone (otherwise the easing tail of an interface or plugin group would be judged settled and flattened early), and the debug counter gains a `plugin` column. ④ **The panel is rearranged**: `src/ext-parallax.js` uses a new helper `parallaxSection(label, key, rows)` to build three cards (reusing `we-picker__section` / `-head` / `-label`, the same shape as the "Picture" card): **Background** (background travel + mascot follow) / **Native front end** (interface follow + the four region distances) / **Plugin front end** (one `0–10 / 0.1` row per discovered slot, labelled with the slot key; a hint when none was found), with the global "easing smoothness" still last; a plugin row's readout goes through the new helper `parallaxPluginPercent(v)` (`null` / `undefined` / `""` / non-numeric ⇒ the default, an explicit 0 kept) and that default is **inlined from the layer** (so `src/parallax-layer.js` exports `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT` as a fourth name — the panel must not copy the number); the list reaches the island through `extensionCtx()`'s `parallaxPluginSlots` via `parallaxDiscoveredGroups()` (**one source of truth**: one row per discovered group), and neither the layer nor the panel looks for plugin groups while interface follow is off.
  **Why**: ① user wording ① asks that with the cursor in a screen corner the displacement be `p%` x diagonal, while the old `pct/100 x u` only reached `pct%` after the cursor travelled **half** a diagonal ⇒ divisor 100 → 50 (a x2 gain), and the wallpaper overscan must move with it or the displacement would exceed the enlargement and show the base colour. ② "panel x100 / stored ÷100" was the debt the previous entry took on to avoid one migration (see the next entry); since the slider domain had to change anyway (0–3 factor → 0–10 percentage) it is paid off here, and retiring the total factor removes a second multiplication — each region storing its own absolute distance is the only form in which user ruling ③'s "0 means that element does not ease" becomes literally true (under the old chain a total factor of 0 froze all four regions, yet the panel could not show *which* region was 0). ③ Plugin groups can only be found by **reading the DOM**: ownership is unavailable at runtime (an occupant's `registrant` is always `mf`, child-slot registrations do not bubble up, and the host docs call slot information a development-time tool — the evidence is collected in `DSH-UI-INTERFACES.md` §3.6), and this plugin already anchors on `[data-slot="…"]`; the cost is that the host's own interface slots show up under "Plugin front end" too, where the user can set them to 0. ④ The three cards are the literal wording of user point ③. **Rejected**: multiplying the plugin distance by a total factor as well (that would bring back both the retired second multiplication and the question of what 0 means), and listing plugins from the slot registry grouped by package name (ownership cannot be read, and a package name is someone else's free naming).
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (465)`** (⚠️ the count is now **466** per the entry above, where the `onSlots` case was renamed `onUiSlots` and tightened to "interface on + plugin off ⇒ not a single plugin row may appear", and an `onPlugin` case was added) — the export check becomes "four names" (`PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT,disposeParallaxLayer,parallaxDiscoveredGroups,syncParallaxLayer`, plus `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT === 1` and `parallaxDiscoveredGroups()` returning an array), and anchors are added for the unit (`PARALLAX_STEP_DIV = 50` and both new formulas), the four group constants, `PARALLAX_GROUP_DEPTH_MAX = 10`, the six plugin constants and the three new functions, `selection.parallaxPluginDepths`, `function parallaxMaxPercent(st)`, `parallaxTargetAdd(…, kind, slot)`, `return coef * PARALLAX_UI_SIGN;` together with the plugin branch `return parallaxClamp(st.plugin[rec.slot], …)`, and `scale: calc(1 + var(--we-parallax-bg, 0) / 50)`, plus two negative anchors (the source must **no longer** contain `PARALLAX_UI_DEPTH` / `st.uiDepth` / `const pctMax = st.bg;`); the fake-DOM harness keeps running with a mixed fixture (the four regions each moving by their own absolute distance 1% / 1.5% / 0.6% / 0.4%, with "one region set to 0 leaves it completely still" now phrased as "set the conversation text area distance to 0"), and the island gains an `onSlots` case (two slot keys ⇒ two extra rows, row label = the slot key) plus a source-anchor check for `parallaxPluginPercent()`. `test/verify-client.mjs` asserts by walking the tree that the three cards appear in order inside the "extension modules" container, that the four region sliders have domain `0..10` and read out `1%` (⚠️ the readout is now pinned per row at 1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4), that the "interface follow distance" row is gone for good, and that the "no other plugin's front-end element groups found yet" state is reachable (that file's fake DOM has no `querySelectorAll`, so it is the only state it can see; ⚠️ that assertion now requires switching the plugin switch on first — see the entry above). `test/verify-i18n.mjs` green across all 41 checks (the "interface follow distance" entry and its tooltip are deleted, the four region tooltips are rewritten to "as a share of the screen's longest diagonal / 0 = this group does not follow", and the three card titles plus the two plugin-section hints are added, Chinese and English in step; `%` is not in the copy table). `test/fixtures/settings-sanitize-golden.json` regenerated from the code (18 cases: `parallaxUiDepth` gone, the four region keys re-valued, `parallaxPluginDepths` added, **zero drift on every other key**); `lib/client.js` rebuilt (2240610 bytes); four documents follow (`HOW-IT-WORKS` in both languages / `CODE-STRUCTURE` / `DSH-UI-INTERFACES` §3.6).

- **Two small "3D depth" fixes — every easing distance in the settings panel is now shown as a percentage, and interface follow no longer grows a horizontal scrollbar on the conversation scroll container (the real source of the shiver across the centre line)** (user report, two points: ① "about the shiver across the centre line — when the cursor moves from the right half to the left half, a black bar shows up at the bottom of the composer and pushes it up, and that is where the shiver comes from"; ② "I want every easing setting in the settings panel to use percentages as its unit").
  **What**: ① **The black bar / shiver**: interface follow moves the **real elements inside the conversation scroll container** (`[data-slot="conversation.view"]` / `[data-composer-card]` / user bubbles), and once the horizontal displacement crosses that scroller's inline-end, the `overflow-y: auto` the host writes on it makes the other axis' `overflow-x: visible` compute to `auto` (spec: when one axis is not `visible`, the other axis' `visible` computes to `auto`) ⇒ a **horizontal scrollbar** appears. It eats roughly one scrollbar's worth of scrollport, so the sticky composer can only move up with it — which is exactly "a black bar at the bottom of the composer pushing it up"; as the cursor crosses the screen centre the displacement reverses ⇒ the scrollbar comes and goes ⇒ the text area and the composer shiver together (the quantisation hysteresis added earlier was only a secondary contributor). `src/styles.js`'s parallax section gains `body[data-we-parallax="on"] [data-conversation-scroll] { overflow-x: hidden; }`: conversation content never scrolls sideways anyway (long tokens and wide code blocks scroll inside their own boxes) ⇒ the axis is sealed and the scrollbar never gets a chance to appear, so the scrollport height stays put. ② **Units** (⚠️ the chain this section described at the time — **panel x100 / stored value stays a factor** — was retired wholesale by the entry above: the panel and the stored value are now both "the absolute percentage of the screen's longest diagonal", and the `parallaxUiDepth` total factor is gone; this section is kept for the motivation and the cost assessment of that moment): the four region distances went from "a 0–3 factor shown as a bare number" to **shown as a percentage in the panel** (0%–300%, step 5%): the four slider rows in `src/ext-parallax.js` get a domain of the setting domain x100 (`Math.round(sel.parallaxUi*Depth * 100)`) with a **bare** `"%"` unit (only the bare-unit form offers the in-place readout while dragging; the pre-formatted-string form does not follow the drag — see `SliderRow`'s `readout` / `preformatted`), and the four named handlers in `src/client.js` become `commitLiveSetting("parallaxUi*Depth", v / 100, live)` — the same chain as "dim" and "border emphasis" (panel x100, stored value stays a fraction). **The stored unit is unchanged**: still a `num 0..3` factor ⇒ no migration, no `settingsVersion` bump, zero fixture drift, and no host restart (client-only change).
  **Why**: ① which element the displacement lands on is decided by the host DOM — we cannot change its anchor, but we *can* stop it from being pushed into a scrollbar, and sealing the axis is the only form where the scrollbar "never gets a chance to appear" (it is safer than `::-webkit-scrollbar:horizontal { display: none }`, which would switch that container to custom scrollbars and change the look of the vertical one too); moving the displacement onto another container was **rejected** — that gives up "the whole block moves together" and the text would come apart from the glass. ② Every other easing row in the panel (`background travel` / `interface travel` / `easing smoothness`) already speaks `%`; only these four were bare factors, and the user asked for the panel to be **uniform**; making the *stored* value a percentage too would mean touching KINDS plus a `settingsVersion` migration plus regenerating the fixture (34 version-number sites) — not worth it for a display unit, and it would make the host's old whitelist treat the new unit as invalid, forcing a desktop restart.
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` — the parallax CSS check gains the anchor `body[data-we-parallax="on"] [data-conversation-scroll] { overflow-x: hidden; }` (with the causal comment), and the third-module island gains a check: each of the four slider rows must carry `, 0, 300, 5, Math.round(sel.parallaxUi*Depth * 100)` and `, "%", "parallax-ui-*-depth"`, the four handlers must be the `v / 100` form, and the old forms (`sel.parallaxUi*Depth, onParallaxUi*Depth` / `commitLiveSetting("parallaxUi*Depth", v, live)`) must be **gone** ⇒ `ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (464)` (⚠️ that group of anchors — `0, 300, 5` / `v / 100` / "the old forms must be gone" — was replaced wholesale by the entry above and now stands at 465 checks); `test/verify-i18n.mjs` green across all 41 checks (the shipping values inside the four tooltips become 100% / 150% / 60% / 40%, Chinese and English in step); the DEFAULTS / KINDS comments in `lib/settings-schema.js` state "stored as a factor, shown as a percentage"; `lib/client.js` rebuilt.

- **The "3D depth" interface follow became adjustable — one distance factor per region, no more shivering as the cursor crosses the screen centre, and the top-left "collapse sidebar" button is no longer pushed down while the sidebar is expanded** (user report, three points: ① the easing distance of each region should be adjustable on its own; ② when the cursor moves from one half of the screen to the other, the central text area and the bottom composer shiver; ③ the "collapse sidebar" button in the very top-left corner is displaced downwards while the sidebar is expanded, and is fine once it is collapsed). **⚠️ The setting unit described in this entry was superseded wholesale by the "easing distance unified as … percentage" entry above** (the four region keys and `parallaxUiDepth` are now "the absolute percentage of the screen's longest diagonal", slider domain `0–10`; the `num 0..3` / shipping 1 / 1.5 / 0.6 / 0.4 written below is only how it looked then). **The defects recorded here still matter**: `parallaxSnapAxis()` hysteresis, the left column's relative positioning and the `parallaxGroupBox()` start moving to the element itself **are all still the current implementation** (current shipping values 1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4).
  **What**: ① **A factor per region**: four new setting keys (`lib/settings-schema.js`, DEFAULTS + KINDS, `num 0..3`) — `parallaxUiChatDepth` / `parallaxUiComposerDepth` / `parallaxUiSidebarDepth` / `parallaxUiBubbleDepth`, shipping with the four coefficients that used to be hard-coded in the layer (1 / 1.5 / 0.6 / 0.4); `parallaxUiDepth` becomes the **total factor**, and the effective coefficient is `uiDepth x region x PARALLAX_UI_SIGN` (so old settings keep their meaning and the look does not change), while the layer's `PARALLAX_GROUP_CHAT / COMPOSER / SIDEBAR / BUBBLE` constants drop to "fallback when the setting cannot be read". `src/ext-parallax.js` gains four sliders after "interface follow distance" (conversation text area / composer / sidebar / user bubble, 0-3 in steps of 0.1), `src/client.js` gains four named handlers (`onParallaxUi*Depth`) wired into `ctx`, and `src/i18n-copy.js` gains the matching labels and tooltips. ② **The shiver**: `parallaxSnap()` rounded by `devicePixelRatio`, while the easing tail lingers near zero — once the target lands in the 0.25-0.75 device-pixel band, `Math.round` flips 0<->+-1 from frame to frame, so `translate` is repeatedly removed and re-added and the whole block (plus the composer, which follows the same scroll container) jumps in steps (the wallpaper layer is not snapped, which is why only the interface shivered). It becomes `parallaxSnapAxis(v, prev)`: **per-record hysteresis** (dead band `PARALLAX_GROUP_DEAD_PX = 0.25`, re-trip at `PARALLAX_GROUP_STICK_PX = 0.75`), with the record storing the **whole device pixel** it last wrote, so the value only changes once it leaves the hysteresis band; the discipline of "remove `translate` entirely once it settles" stays. ③ **The displaced collapse button**: the sidebar group's landing target is the **parent** of the `[data-slot="sidebar"]` outlet (that column), and in Windows titlebar mode the host's collapse button is a **`position: fixed` descendant of that very column** ⇒ writing `translate` on the column makes it the button's containing block and shifts the button down by a whole titlebar height (the same class of accident as #131); it used to be wrong **only while expanded** because the expanded subtree exceeds `PARALLAX_GROUP_SCAN_MAX = 400` and `parallaxGroupBlocked()` lets it through (that comment literally says an over-limit scan counts as "move it anyway"), while the collapsed subtree is small and is judged blocked. The fix: the left column **switches to relative positioning** (`position: relative` plus `left` / `top`, **never** `translate`) and is **exempt** from that fixed-descendant rule (relative positioning establishes no containing block, so it is safe by construction). ④ Along the way `parallaxGroupBox()` now starts at **`el` itself** instead of `el.parentElement` (if it owns a box, use it) ⇒ the composer lands on the card itself and the 24 bubbles each become their own displacement target (they used to land on the conversation text area's box); the self-check line gains an "offset" count for the left column (`parallaxGroupCounts()`'s `offset`).
  **Why**: ① those four factors were hard-coded and the user asked for them to be adjustable; keeping `parallaxUiDepth` as a total factor means no migration for existing settings and an unchanged look (the new keys ship at the old constants). ② Snapping cannot go away (half pixels blur text), so the shiver has to be fixed at "do not flip around zero": hysteresis is the minimal change for exactly that shape; it is not "always keep `translate: 0px 0px`" because the property alone establishes a containing block. ③ Relative positioning is the only form that moves that column without re-anchoring a `fixed` descendant inside it, at the price of one layout pass per frame on that column (the sidebar is small, so that is affordable); the conservative alternative ("treat an over-limit scan as blocked") was **rejected** — it would freeze the conversation text area in long sessions, a regression.
  **Guards**: the parallax source-anchor block in `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` re-anchors (`function parallaxSnapAxis(v, prev)` / the two hysteresis constants / the four region keys and `parallaxTargetRatio()`'s `st.chatDepth` branches / `parallaxGroupBox()`'s `let node = el;` / `parallaxGroupOffsets(kind)` / `parallaxTargetIsOffset(rec)` / `parallaxTargetOffset(rec, x, y)` / `blocked: isGroup && !offsets ? … : false,` / the `PARALLAX_POSITION_RELATIVE` line inside `parallaxTargetOffset`), and the fake-DOM case gains left-column assertions (the column must carry **no** `translate` and must carry `position: relative` with `left` / `top`; once settled `left` / `top` are removed while `position` **stays**; the expanded sidebar holding a **`fixed` descendant** still moves), a hysteresis assertion (after settling, move the cursor until the target is only 0.4 device pixels away and that whole pixel must be **kept**, not removed) and a per-region assertion (setting the conversation text area factor to 0 leaves the text area completely still while the column still offsets), plus the four new slider copy anchors in the third-module island; the settings fixture `test/fixtures/settings-sanitize-golden.json` takes the four keys into both sides (safety valve = zero drift on every other key, regenerated by `.test-cache/regen-golden-uidepth.mjs`); the Chinese and English `HOW-IT-WORKS` / `CODE-STRUCTURE` / `README` / `DSH-UI-INTERFACES` follow. `npm run verify:all` green.

- **Fix + extension: the "3D depth" interface follow — only the input box moved, the direction was the opposite of the wallpaper's, and user bubbles had no easing of their own** (user report, three points: ① visually only the wallpaper and the bottom input box move, though the effect is nice; ② the input box and the wallpaper should move **the same way**, to read as layers with depth; ③ give the sidebar (the left one first — the right one comes from the sidebar module), the central text area (where the conversation is shown) and the user bubbles inside it **independent** easing).
  **What**: three changes in `src/parallax-layer.js`. ① **The displacement now lands on the nearest ancestor that owns a box**: the host's slot renderer hard-codes `const ANCHOR_STYLE = { display: "contents" }` for every outlet (its comment: ``display:contents` keeps the wrapper out of layout … so the anchor is purely addressable surface.`), and an element with `display: contents` **generates no box** ⇒ writing `translate` on `[data-slot="conversation.view"]` / `[data-slot="sidebar"]` moves nothing on screen — that is why "only the input box moved" (`[data-composer-card]` is a real boxed card). The new `parallaxGroupBox(el)` walks up from the outlet's `parentElement` to the first ancestor with `display !== 'contents'` (cap `PARALLAX_GROUP_BOX_MAX_UP = 3`; when that parent is `body` / `documentElement` it returns null — better not to move at all; without `getComputedStyle` it returns the parent directly), and only on the re-scan path (outside the frame, throttled to 250ms). ② **The direction is now the same as the wallpaper's**: `PARALLAX_UI_FLIP` is gone, `PARALLAX_UI_SIGN = 1` replaces it, and `parallaxTargetRatio()` answers `st.uiDepth * coef * PARALLAX_UI_SIGN;`. ③ **User bubbles ease on their own**: the selector gains `[data-chat-flow-kind="user"]` / `[data-chat-flow-kind="steering"]` (the bubble's box carries a row of semantic data attributes written by the host, while its class names are build hashes and unreliable), `parallaxGroupKind()` recognises it first and answers `'bubble'`, the factor `PARALLAX_GROUP_BUBBLE = 0.4` **stacks on top of the conversation text area** (1.4x in total), and the nested-group pass **lets bubbles through** (every other group-in-group case still keeps only the outermost one); the new `PARALLAX_GROUP_BUBBLE_MAX = 24` means only the **24 most recent** bubbles in DOM order receive a displacement. The self-check line gains an interface-group census (new `parallaxGroupCounts()`: chat / composer / sidebar / bubble / blocked by `fixed`), exposed as `window.__weParallaxStats.groups`. Three copies in `src/i18n-copy.js` (module description, sub-switch hint, distance tooltip) plus the comments in `src/ext-parallax.js` and `src/client.js` follow. **`src/styles.js` did not change at all.**
  **Why**: ① this is the host contract (outlets exist only to be addressable and deliberately stay out of layout), so the displacement has to go to the real box underneath them — which also explains why only the composer could follow. ② The user asked for the input box and the wallpaper to move the same way "to read as layers with depth": same direction plus different factors is what a camera pan looks like (the near interface travels further than the far wallpaper), whereas the opposite direction reads as "the interface floats in front" and is not the request. ③ Bubbles are the layer closest to the eye, so a little extra displacement buys depth; but their number is unbounded (a long conversation has hundreds) and writing `translate` on each one costs real frame budget, hence the cap of the 24 most recent.
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` re-anchors (`PARALLAX_UI_SIGN` replaces `PARALLAX_UI_FLIP`, the five-part selector string, `PARALLAX_GROUP_BUBBLE` / `_BUBBLE_MAX` / `_BOX_MAX_UP`, `function parallaxGroupBox(el)` / `parallaxGroupCounts()`, the new `parallaxTargetAdd(next, prev, el, group, inFrame, kind)` signature, `if (nested && candidates[i].kind !== 'bubble') continue;`); the fake-DOM case gives every outlet a `display: contents` outlet plus a boxed parent (asserting the displacement lands on the **parent box** while the outlet itself carries **no** `translate`) and adds 30 bubbles (asserting only the 24 most recent move and `dbg.groups.bubble === 24`), and the direction assertion flips from "opposite to the wallpaper" to **the same as** it; the third-module copy in `test/verify-client.mjs` follows. `npm run verify:all` green.

- **Added: "3D depth" can now carry the interface along — the composer / conversation text area / sidebar drift as whole blocks, the text moving in and out together with the glass underneath** (user request: extend the 3D feel to the input box / text area / sidebar, with the text itself moving together with the glass elements — a combined motion). **⚠️ This entry was superseded wholesale by the two entries above**: `parallaxUiDepth` (the interface-follow total factor) has been **deleted** (`grep` finds 0 hits in `lib/settings-schema.js` and `src/ext-parallax.js`), the four region keys became independent absolute percentages, KINDS went from `num 0..6` / `0..3` to `num 0..10`, and the shipping values are now 1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4. What follows is kept as the design and reasoning of that moment (the conclusions about direction, whole-block displacement and not touching `src/styles.js` still hold).
  **What**: two new setting keys (`lib/settings-schema.js`, DEFAULTS + KINDS) — `parallaxUi` (`boolFalse`, **off by default**: it moves the real interface and must not repaint everyone's screen) and `parallaxUiDepth` (`num 0..6`, default `1`: the conversation text area is the base, the composer x1.5, the sidebar x0.6). The behaviour layer `src/parallax-layer.js` gains a third leg: the interface group selector `PARALLAX_GROUP_SELECTOR` (five parts: `[data-composer-card]` / `[data-slot="conversation.view"]` / `[data-slot="sidebar"]` / `[data-chat-flow-kind="user"]` / `[data-chat-flow-kind="steering"]`), the four group factors (`PARALLAX_GROUP_CHAT = 1` / `PARALLAX_GROUP_COMPOSER = 1.5` / `PARALLAX_GROUP_SIDEBAR = 0.6` / `PARALLAX_GROUP_BUBBLE = 0.4`) and the direction constant `PARALLAX_UI_SIGN = 1` (the interface moves the **same way as** the wallpaper, so it reads as a camera pan; the first version used `PARALLAX_UI_FLIP = 1` for the opposite direction and was changed per the user's wording — see the entry above). What moves is the **container as a whole** (that is the only way the text travels with the glass and stays sharp); because the host's slot outlets (`[data-slot=…]`) hard-code `display: contents` and generate no box, the displacement lands on the **nearest ancestor that owns one** (`parallaxGroupBox()`, at most 3 levels up) — otherwise nothing moves on screen (see the entry above). That is also why the layer grows `parallaxTargetAdd()`'s de-duplication plus a `contains()` pass that **drops nested groups** (only the outermost one moves, otherwise the displacements would multiply; **user bubbles are the deliberate exception** — they live inside the conversation text area's box, so their displacement **stacks** on top of it). Interface groups have three rules of their own: ① the displacement is **snapped to whole device pixels** (`parallaxSnap()` rounds by `devicePixelRatio` — half pixels blur text); ② once it settles, the new `parallaxTargetUnset()` **removes the `translate` property entirely** (the property alone establishes a containing block and re-anchors fixed-position descendants — that is the #89 trap); ③ a group holding any `position: fixed` descendant (`parallaxGroupBlocked()`, scanning at most 400 nodes) **stays put as a whole**. Interface groups are **never promoted to compositor layers** (`parallaxTargetsSettle()` skips them; `will-change` stays with the wallpaper and the mascot), and the subtree scan is **never redone inside a frame** (a group that appears mid-frame starts out static and is judged on a later frame). The descriptor `src/ext-parallax.js` gains two rows: the "Interface follows" sub-switch and the "Interface travel" slider (0–6%, step 0.5, drawn only while the sub-switch is on); `src/client.js` gains the named handlers `onParallaxUi` / `onParallaxUiDepth` wired into `ctx`; `src/i18n-copy.js` follows with six entries (including the master hint, now "the interface itself stays put unless you turn on Interface follows below"); the module description mentions the interface blocks. **`src/styles.js` is untouched by a single line** — this leg adds no CSS at all, the displacement is computed in JS and merely lends each element its own independent `translate` property.
  **Why**: the user asked for the text itself to move with the glass, and the only form that does not blur is to move the **whole container** (per-glyph or per-node motion is both expensive and blurry); the three factors exist to give the layers a little depth (composer nearest, sidebar farthest), and the direction is flipped because the interface sits above the wallpaper. Off by default, plus a sub-switch of its own: it moves the **real interface** (an input box sits right where the cursor is, far more noticeable than the wallpaper), so the factory default must not repaint anyone's screen — consistent with the upstream stance that effect features ship off.
  **Guards**: the parallax source-anchor block in `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` gains about 22 interface-group anchors (the selector, the four factors, `PARALLAX_UI_SIGN`, `parallaxSnap`, `parallaxGroupBlocked`, `parallaxTargetUnset`, `if (rec.group) continue;`, the in-frame flag of `parallaxTargetsRefresh(now, inFrame)`), and the fake-DOM case now **routes by selector** and adds interface-group assertions (all three displacements are integers, their sign matches the wallpaper layer, the sidebar travels less than the conversation text area, the nested group and the composer group holding a fixed descendant carry **no** `translate`, none of the four groups carries the compositor-layer class, and after the pointer returns to the exact screen center all four groups' `translate` is **removed entirely**); the third-module block in `test/verify-client.mjs` covers the new switch, the 0..6 slider range, "while it is off not even the copy appears" and "turning the sub-switch or the master switch off collapses those two rows again"; the settings fixture `test/fixtures/settings-sanitize-golden.json` takes both keys into the client and host expectations (safety valve = zero drift on every other key); the Chinese / English `README`, `HOW-IT-WORKS` and `CODE-STRUCTURE` follow (and the stale "only writes CSS variables / frames capped at 60Hz" wording in the README now describes the current mechanism). `npm run verify:all` green.

- **Optimisation: the 3D depth effect's per-frame cost drops another notch — the displacement no longer travels through an inherited custom property, each frame writes `translate` on the layers themselves** (user report: much smoother after the metrics module retired, but CPU usage still fluctuates noticeably while moving the cursor quickly).
  **What**: the change lives in `src/parallax-layer.js` (behaviour layer), the parallax section of `src/styles.js` and the injection marker in `scripts/build-client.mjs` — **no new setting key, no new copy, not a pixel of UI moved**. ① **The write mechanism changed**: the per-frame "displacement step" used to be a CSS custom property `--we-parallax-x / -y` (multiplied by the per-layer factors inside the stylesheet with `calc()`), and **custom properties inherit** — one write puts a whole subtree into "style needs re-resolving", 60 times a second while the cursor moves; now each frame writes the **final, fully multiplied displacement** with `style.setProperty('translate', 'X.XXpx Y.XXpx')` straight onto `.we-layer` / `.we-rope`'s own **CSS independent property `translate`** (two decimals; nothing is written when the value did not change, compared against a snapshot the layer keeps itself rather than reading the DOM), so there are **zero custom-property writes per frame**; the body keeps a single "wallpaper bleed" multiplier `--we-parallax-bg` (written once per settings change) feeding the unchanged static `scale: calc(1 + var(--we-parallax-bg, 0) / 50) (⚠️ this entry was written when it was `/ 100`; the divisor became `50` in the "percentage" entry above — recorded here at the current value)`. ② **The artificial 60Hz cap is gone** (frames follow the display's real refresh rate; the easing already converted by the real dt, so the feel is unchanged and high-refresh displays no longer skip every other frame). ③ **Zero measurements inside the frame**: the viewport is read when the loop starts and on resize, target re-scanning moved out of the frame onto the start path and is throttled to 250ms, and frames no longer call `querySelectorAll` or read `window.innerWidth`. ④ **Nothing is scheduled while the page is hidden** (`document.hidden` returns immediately, `visibilitychange` picks the loop back up). ⑤ A new **self-check switch**: `localStorage.weParallaxDebug = '1'` records per-frame callback cost / write count / frame gap and prints one line with p50 / p95 / max plus a long-frame (≥8ms) count when the gesture settles, exposed as `window.__weParallaxStats`.
  **Why**: the user's report was "much smoother after the metrics module retired, but CPU usage still fluctuates noticeably" — the remaining big item is exactly that inherited custom-property write: its cost does not scale with "how many elements move" but with **the size of the subtree that inherits it** (sidebar + conversation, thousands of nodes), while the displacement only affects the presentation of those few elements, so writing it on themselves is enough. The in-frame measurements (viewport / target re-scan) also interleaved layout queries with style writes, which is the shape of "an occasional hitch".
  **Guards**: the parallax source-anchor block in `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` re-anchors (adds `const PARALLAX_TRANSLATE = 'translate';`, `const PARALLAX_VAR_BG = '--we-parallax-bg';`, `parallaxVarOn(el, PARALLAX_TRANSLATE, value);`, `function parallaxApply(st)` / `parallaxTargetKind` / `parallaxTargetsEnsure` / `parallaxHidden` / `parallaxDebugSync` / `parallaxDebugFrame` / `parallaxDebugReport`, the passive `visibilitychange` listener; drops `PARALLAX_VAR_X / -Y`, `PARALLAX_MIN_FRAME_MS` and the three in-frame cap/write lines, and adds `--we-parallax-x / -y` as negative assertions), the CSS-section check follows (only that bleed `scale` and `will-change: translate` remain; `--we-parallax-x / -y / -mascot` must be gone), the fake-DOM runtime case now asserts the **element's own `translate`** and a body carrying only `--we-parallax-bg`, plus a new self-check assertion (with `localStorage.weParallaxDebug` on, `window.__weParallaxStats` carries frame statistics when the gesture settles); the injection marker in `scripts/build-client.mjs` follows (a missing marker refuses the build). The Chinese and English HOW-IT-WORKS / CODE-STRUCTURE pages follow (the behaviour layer's "two legs" wording changes from "writes CSS variables" to "writes the independent property directly").

- **New: titlebar liquid glass — the same glass the left sidebar gets** (user request: "glass the title bar, make it look like the left sidebar's liquid glass, with no colour difference").
  **What**: new setting key `titlebarGlass` (off by default; type-B, so off restores the shell's own opaque background), four rows added to the Glass UI panel in the same group as the left-sidebar one (master switch + titlebar independent-config + transparency/frost sliders); `GLASS_CHILDREN` registers it with `panelOff: true`, `DEFAULTS` / `KINDS` / the preset fixed-key table follow ⇒ **zero migration** for existing configs and factory presets. The title bar is **not an element** — it is the AppFrame grid container's pseudo-element (`[data-windows-titlebar] .pI_x6G_frame:before`); the shell class name the first version hung on, `.dshDesktopFrameTitlebar`, appears **0 times** in a literal scan of the whole app.asar (the switch flipped, the variables were right, and the CSS simply never matched), so the anchor became `div[class*="pI_x6G_frame"]` (a **substring** match on the hash class — if it drifts across versions the worst case is "this block does nothing", never collateral damage), plus two shape gates: `html[data-windows-titlebar]` and `data-we-adapter^="desktop-"`.
  **Why**: ① background and blur must live on **two separate pseudo-elements** — background/sheen/accent stay on the shell's `::before`, `backdrop-filter` moves to a new `::after` (aligned geometry, `pointer-events:none` so it never blocks `-webkit-app-region: drag`); the left sidebar is "background on the element, blur on its `::before` above it" ⇒ `F(wallpaper ⊕ background)`, and putting both in one pseudo-element yields `background ⊕ F(wallpaper)` where the background never passes saturate/brightness ⇒ **the numbers can be identical and the colour still differs**. ② The sheen becomes a **constant `sheen-a`**: the stops are percentages, so the same three-stop gradient put in a 40px box and a 1111.33px box averages 0.0495 vs 0.0967 with a 0.05 step at the seam. ③ **No bottom hairline** — the same policy the left sidebar already follows via S2 (the line itself is a colour difference). ④ **A selector list fails as a whole**: pairing an unverified nested `:has()` seat anchor with the hash anchor in one comma list makes the shell's Chromium reject the former ⇒ the entire rule is dropped **including the one that matched**, which looks exactly like "wrong anchor" — static scans, regex guards and css-select all miss it.
  **Guards**: `verify-glass-compositing` gains TB1–TB6 (dead anchor / two-layer split / shell gate / six identical colour declarations / constant sheen / no hairline), with negative controls that exercise the **real predicate** rather than literals; `verify-glass-surfaces` registers the `titlebar-override` surface and the `--we-titlebar-*` private variable group; `verify-presets` fixed-key table, `settings-sanitize-golden` re-recorded (3 keys × 18 cases × 2 sides).

- **Post-delivery audit round: two real defects (A1 / A6) + one same-origin regression (F1) + a batch of stale comments and documentation claims, with CI reproduced step by step locally and fully green** (independent audit after the five entries above and the titlebar glass landed: the parent agent read the behaviour layer line by line, two further audits read styles/glass and docs/comments; **no** factory switch changed, **no** unit changed, **no** `settingsVersion` bump).
  **What**: ① **A1**: `parallaxStop()`'s closing self-check always reported 0 groups — it called `parallaxDebugReport()` *after* `parallaxTargetsClear()`, and the group count is read off exactly that cleared table; the group counts are now snapshotted before the clear (`parallaxDebugOn ? parallaxGroupCounts() : null`) and `parallaxDebugReport(groupCounts)` receives them (no cost when the debug switch is off, printing unchanged). ② **A6**: `parallaxPluginEffectiveGroups()` listed a row per slot key even when several of them end up on the **same landing box**, while `parallaxTargetAdd()` (`src/parallax-layer.js:740`) dedupes by `el` on its first line ⇒ **only the first record on screen ever receives a displacement**, yet the panel drew every row — breaking the hard invariant "every row the panel lists has a landing / the panel list and what moves on screen are the same set" (both functions' comments state it as an invariant); the function now dedupes by **landing box** at the end (`const boxes = []; … if (!box || boxes.indexOf(box) >= 0) continue; … return kept;`), purely additive with zero behaviour change (the dropped records were already dropped by `parallaxTargetAdd()`). ③ **F1**: the dark-theme titlebar `::before` still used **three gradient stops** (`…sheen-a 0% / …sheen-b 38% / …sheen-c 100%`) after the light path had been moved to the single stop `sheen-a` — the same table, the same box-size colour-difference regression growing back; and `verify-glass-compositing`'s `tbLightRule` explicitly excludes `data-ds-dark-theme` ⇒ **zero coverage on the dark half**. The dark half is now a single `sheen-a` stop too, with a new **TB4b** pair (dark sheen constant + a negative control demanding that three stops fail *and* that the light/dark sheen declarations are word-for-word the same). ④ **Stale comments and documentation** (8 places): `src/glass.js:196-199` / `lib/settings-schema.js:256-264` and `:545-551` still named the vanished class `.dshDesktopFrameTitlebar` / variable `--dsh-desktop-frame-fill` and a "titlebar bottom hairline" (the anchor is now the `div[class*="pI_x6G_frame"]` hash substring, and the hairline is deliberately absent); `src/glass.js:104` and two spots in `src/effects.js` still said "slider 0–60" (the range has been 0–100); `src/styles.js:3257` pointed at "the bleed rule above" while the rule sits *below* the comment; `README.md:121` / `README.en.md:128` still spoke of "a total factor with four sliders under it" and "crossing that whole diagonal" (the `parallaxUiDepth` total factor is retired; the meaning is "the cursor parked in a screen corner"); `docs/DSH-UI-INTERFACES.md` §2.2 gained the `data-windows-titlebar` shape gate and §2.3 gained a third shape, the **full hash substring**, together with its **ratchet blind spot** (`test/compat-harness-surfaces.mjs` only extracts `[class*="_xxx"]` suffixes, so `pI_x6G` is invisible to it and only `verify-glass-surfaces`' `anchors` covers it); `docs/CODE-STRUCTURE.md` §3.1 registers the `glass-panel` / `glass` / `preset-store` modules; `docs/en/HOW-IT-WORKS.md` stops calling 3D depth "the second module" (it is the **third** entry in the registry) and fixes the indentation and two split lines at `:198-210`; both changelogs annotate two **superseded** r2 entries with ⚠️ notes (`parallaxUiDepth` deleted, KINDS `num 0..6` → `num 0..10`, shipping defaults now 1.2 / 1.8 / 1.6 / 1.4) and record the superseded entries' `scale: … / 100` at the **current** `/ 50`.
  **Why**: every item here is either "a reader following the comment/doc would get it wrong" or "a guard that looks like it has teeth but only covers half"; A1 / A6 are real defects in the behaviour layer (a self-check that always reports zero, a panel list that is not the set of things that move) and F1 is a just-fixed same-origin regression growing back. **Deliberately unchanged**: `PARALLAX_GROUP_SCAN_MAX = 400` "past the cap, let it move", the plugin depth table only ever growing, the generic `map` sanitize, and "the panel list is a render-time snapshot" (reasons in this section's "Audit round" entry).
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` ⇒ **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (472)`** — the existing A1 structural guard was itself **stale** (it pinned `function parallaxDebugReport()` and had to go red once the signature took a parameter, i.e. the guard needed upgrading, not the implementation), now three guards (signature takes `groupCounts` / the snapshot line **must precede `parallaxTargetsClear();`** / the call site passes the snapshot), plus three A6 anchors (`const boxes = [];` / `if (!box || boxes.indexOf(box) >= 0) continue;` / `return kept;`). `test/verify-glass-compositing.mjs` ⇒ `ALL GLASS COMPOSITING CHECKS PASSED` (the new TB4b pair was shown to tell good from bad). `lib/settings-schema.js` changed comments only (zero change to keys or values). `lib/client.js` rebuilt: `test/verify-client-sync.mjs` ⇒ `CLIENT SYNC CHECKS PASSED (4)`; with `core.autocrlf` off, `git diff --exit-code -- lib/client.js` is exit 0 on a **clean HEAD tree**, and stays exit 0 after a rebuild ⇒ the builder is idempotent with zero byte drift. `verify:docs` five scripts green, `npm run smoke` exit 0, `git diff --check` exit 0. **CI equivalence**: every step of `.github/workflows/verify.yml` reproduced locally (build / verify / verify:bridge --provision / smoke / verify:docs / both diff gates) — **all exit 0**; `verify:bridge` under the relaxed sandbox (the CI posture) is 42 passed / 0 failed / 1 platform-skipped; `test/verify-contracts.mjs` ④⑤ green. The one "red locally, green on CI" case was `test/verify-system-fonts.mjs`'s "real subprocess: multibyte across chunks" — the restricted sandbox forbids piped stdio (`spawn EPERM`), while the relaxed sandbox reports `SYSTEM-FONT CHECKS PASSED (94)` ⇒ it was already green in CI; following the existing `verify-media-bridge` policy it now has an **environment-skip** tier (never silently counted as a pass, never falsely red), so the restricted environment reports `SYSTEM-FONT CHECKS PASSED (93) —— ⚠️ 1 environment skip`. `package.json` untouched, version still `1.3.0-r2`.
- **Follow-up fix round while merging (one should-fix plus three claim mismatches found by the maintainer reviewing PR #147, landed together with the merge)**
  **What**: ① **P1** `src/parallax-layer.js`: `parallaxDebugSync()`'s "re-read at most once per second" throttle had `parallaxDebugOn &&` in its guard — it only short-circuited while the switch was **on**, so in the default state (off) every kick of a gesture hit a synchronous `localStorage` read (60–144 reads/second), contradicting the function's own doc comment; the throttle now ignores the current state (the guard compares time only), a one-line fix. ② **P2** `src/live-layer.js`: the heartbeat self-heal comment promises "no self-heal while paused / hidden", but a **web** wallpaper's `alive` only asks whether the iframe loaded (`iframeLoaded`), which stays true while paused; the heal condition now also requires `isEffectivelyPlaying()` (a paused scene already has `alive === false`, so zero behaviour change there; a paused web page now genuinely does not self-heal and gets healed on the first beat after playback resumes — exactly what the comment always said). ③④ **P2** two stale claims: `lib/settings-schema.js`'s parallax block still described the retired unit ("crossing the whole diagonal" is short by a factor of 2) and "scales by 1+pct%" (now 1+pct/50), and `src/client.js`'s parallax subscription comment still said "only writes CSS variables" and "capped at 60Hz" (it now writes `translate` directly and follows the real refresh rate — and ships inline inside `lib/client.js`); plus `docs/wip/SLIDER-CONSOLIDATION.md` still said verify-system-fonts "always fails with EPERM" (the same tree already carries the environment-skip tier).
  **Why**: P1 is real per-frame cost on the input path (and it hits in the default state); the other three close out under this repo's "a comment/doc claim that contradicts the implementation is a real defect" rule. All four were found reviewing the #147 merge body and do not detract from the three contributors' delivery.
  **Guards**: `npm run verify` / `verify:docs` / `smoke` all exit 0; `lib/client.js` rebuilt and `git diff --exit-code` passes (builder idempotent). **One guard upgraded**: the self-heal structural guard in `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` pinned the old condition string `if (alive && !selection.sceneLiveActive)` verbatim and had to go red once ② changed the condition ⇒ upgraded to the new shape `if (alive && isEffectivelyPlaying() && !selection.sceneLiveActive)` (the negative controls are unchanged: the old branch-condition shape and the unconditional shape must still be rejected), total stays 472. The P1 path has no guard of its own (diagnostics default off, and "how many localStorage reads happened" is not assertable); the comment claim is covered by the file's other structural anchors.

- **P3 clearance round: the oversized group's full enumeration becomes a cooldown cache, the plugin-distance lookups collapse onto one getter, an unreachable dead branch goes away, the dark titlebar gains its same-colour twin check, and both caretakers share one aliveness predicate** (source: the six P3 notes the maintainer listed while reviewing PR #147 — none of them blocked the merge, all were left for a follow-up; **no** factory switch changed, **no** unit changed, **no** `settingsVersion` bump).
  **What**: ① **P3-1 the oversized group stops paying a full enumeration every 250ms** (`src/parallax-layer.js`): the policy of the `PARALLAX_GROUP_SCAN_MAX = 400` perf gate is "could not finish verifying ⇒ let it move", but the old shape **enumerated first, only then discovered the overflow, and let it move** ⇒ in a long conversation that group paid a fresh `querySelectorAll('*')` on every rescan round. New `parallaxGroupNodes(el)` plus `PARALLAX_GROUP_SCAN_COOLDOWN_MS = 1000` and a `WeakMap` cache `parallaxGroupSizes`: a group measured **over the cap** is not even counted for 1s and returns `null` (callers read that as "could not finish verifying" ⇒ word-for-word the old policy); the cache is only updated when a count **really happened** ⇒ the first call after the subtree shrinks back into range re-verifies. `parallaxGroupBlocked()` also drops from two enumerations to one.
  ② **P3-2 one getter for the plugin-distance fallbacks** (same file): `parallaxMaxPercent()`'s pass clamped a non-finite value to **0**, while `parallaxTargetRatio()` did its own `hasOwnProperty` plus a clamp to `PARALLAX_PLUGIN_DEFAULT` ⇒ the same bad value got two different answers, and `pctMax === 0` sent that group's easing down the "settle once and stop" short circuit (the plugin table is a `map` slot — shallow-copied, values unvalidated — so hand-editing `settings.json` reaches it). Both paths now share the new getter `parallaxPluginDepth(st, slot)`: a missing key, a non-finite value and an out-of-range value all give **the same result**.
  ③ **P3-3 an unreachable dead branch is gone** (same file): `parallaxGroupKind()`'s "look for `data-composer-card` in the **className**" line (an attribute name never grows inside a class name) is deleted outright; composer identity is recognised only through `hasAttribute('data-composer-card')`.
  ④ **P3-4 the dark titlebar gets its same-colour check, and a negative control stops being vacuously true** (`test/verify-glass-compositing.mjs`): TB2c's negative control opened with a **literal** tested against `/blur\(/` (always true); it now calls a real predicate `tbBeforeHasBlur(body)` fed a **mutated copy of the real rule**. New **TB4d**: the dark titlebar and the dark left column must match on all **six colour declarations** (background colour + five accent mappings) — TB4's twin comparison had only ever covered the light half and TB4b only covers the sheen, so the dark background colour and accents were zero-covered; the comparison and its negative control share one diff function `darkDiffsOf` (normalising `--we-surface-tint-light` / `-dark` and `rgba(255,255,255,0.04)`).
  ⑤ **P3-5 both caretakers share one aliveness predicate** (`src/live-layer.js`): new module-level `liveHeartbeatAlive(frame, stats, wstate, isWeb)` with the alias `liveHeartbeatReady`. The heartbeat's self-heal judged "still alive" from `iframeLoaded` (web), while `syncLayers()`'s adopt-and-reattach hop used `liveFrameReady()` (which for web needs a readable `getState`) ⇒ a window that is reachable but has not finished loading fell through both and could only wait for the next emit. The two are now **the same source** (the scene branch is word-for-word `liveFrameReady`'s scene semantics ⇒ zero behaviour change on the scene side), and `liveFrameReady` is left only to `scheduleLiveMount()`'s hop.
  ⑥ **A pre-existing defect found on the way: two names in `src/live-layer.js`'s export table were not its own** (`retireFadingLayer` and `nudgeWallpaperRepaint` actually live in `src/layer-core.js`). The inline build strips the whole `export` block and merges every module into one scope ⇒ call sites still resolved and `lib/client.js` was fine, **but "exporting a name you never declared" is a link-time error in any real ESM context**: `node --check src/live-layer.js` had always reported `SyntaxError: Export 'nudgeWallpaperRepaint' is not defined in module`. Both names are dropped from that table (they come from `src/layer-core.js`'s own export table), and the module now parses as real ESM.
  **Why**: ① that gate should save the **enumeration itself**, not just its conclusion; ② one value was being read two ways by two paths (one half trusting the clamp, the other reading straight through), and the worst case is exactly the "that group's easing silently stops working" the maintainer named — all the user sees is "this group stopped moving"; ③ leaving the dead branch in makes a reader believe a class name is also a recognised shape; ④ these are the **looks-like-it-has-teeth** gaps: a negative control measuring a regex with a literal, and a newly added dark-half guard covering only the sheen; ⑤ one semantic had been written inline in two places, and the seam fell exactly on the real time window "reachable but not finished loading"; ⑥ it was "the build happening to cover for the code" — the moment anyone treats `src/live-layer.js` as a real module (or an editor resolves it as ESM), it is a syntax-level error on the spot.
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` ⇒ **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (476)`** (472 → 474 → 476) — three behavioural legs plus two structural counter-checks. Leg ①: an oversized group (`box.querySelectorAll` returning 401 nodes) is counted **exactly once** across two rescans, and must be re-counted after idling 1060ms (measured `{"first":1,"second":1,"after":2,"within":340}`); leg ②: component identity is recognised by attribute only — the same `data-composer-card` group **must pick up its own 1.5% distance the moment its fixed descendant is removed** (proving the earlier stillness came from the fixed test, not from failing to recognise the identity); leg ③: a plugin slot's **non-finite** value and a **missing** key must land on byte-identical displacements side by side, and an **out-of-range** value must clamp to the upper bound (42 ⇒ 10%). Structural counter-check one: `!parSrc.includes("cls.indexOf(' data-composer-card ')")` — that dead branch never holds and reviving it changes no reachable shape, so no behavioural leg can catch it; only a structural guard can. Structural counter-check two: the new `liveFlagChecks.exportListHonest(body)` asks every name in `src/live-layer.js`'s export table "did this file declare you?" (comments are stripped before splitting on commas, otherwise a `// …` line inside the block joins the following names into one chunk and builds an invalid regex), paired with a negative control: synthetic sources that borrow someone else's name — **including the historical shape** `retireFadingLayer, nudgeWallpaperRepaint` — must go red, and a clean table must pass. `test/verify-glass-compositing.mjs` ⇒ `ALL GLASS COMPOSITING CHECKS PASSED` (TB2c now feeds a mutant of the real rule; TB4d's diff algorithm was shown to tell good from bad). **Independent proof of teeth (mutants, `.test-cache/mutate-p3.mjs`)**: A sets the cooldown to `0` ⇒ the behavioural leg goes red; B revives the dead branch and C reverts the "max distance" pass to its old fallback ⇒ the structural guards go red; all three restored ⇒ green. ⚠️ The script once had anchors written with `\n` (this repo has `core.autocrlf=true` ⇒ the files on disk are CRLF), so it **silently did not mutate** and measured the unmutated tree ⇒ a false green; the run now has to be trusted only after checking the `MUTANT …: applied` line. ⑥'s before/after is directly reproducible: before the fix `node --check src/live-layer.js` reported `Export 'nudgeWallpaperRepaint' is not defined in module`; after it the same source parses as real ESM and `names exported but not declared: 0`. `lib/client.js` rebuilt (same commit as `src/**`; `test/verify-client-sync.mjs` ⇒ `CLIENT SYNC CHECKS PASSED (4)`). The full chain `build` / `verify` / `verify:docs` / `smoke` / `verify:bridge` / `git diff --check` is all exit 0. **Client-only change ⇒ refresh the page.**

- **Fix: every plugin reload stacked one more pair of window listeners and one more 60s timer into the same document — the diagnostics and the "restore on restart" listeners move from module scope into `ctx.effect` (fiber scope)** (source: the field evidence gathered while investigating the upstream issue "the composer loses focus every 0.9–3.6s while a wallpaper plays, restored immediately on pause"; the plugin makes **no** periodic focus call anywhere — the focus problem itself is a separate matter — and this is the defect that investigation did prove and that can be fixed on its own. **No** factory switch changed, **no** unit changed, **no** `settingsVersion` bump).
  **What**: ① **Symptom and measurement**: `src/live-layer.js` had four side effects written at **module scope** inside `try{}catch{}` — the boot trace `setTimeout(…, 0)`, `window`'s `focus`/`blur`, `document`'s `visibilitychange`, and the 60s `window.setInterval` `beat`; pointing the new guard at this file then found a fifth: `:1447-1452`, the `window` `pointerdown`/`keydown` pair (`once`) that arms the "restore on restart" tier. The host runs `tearDownEntryFiber` and re-evaluates the module body on every revision change (**metadata-only changes such as mtime/size count too**), and these timers and listeners do not hang off the fiber and are never released ⇒ local diagnostics measured **214 `[we-live d8·pXXXXX]` page ids** inside one document and **one real window blur logged 117 times** by 117 live instances (the 60s beat, and one pixel request per log line to `/diag`, were multiplied ~100× the same way).
  ② **Fix**: new `installLiveDiagnostics()` and `installLiveBootRestore()` (`src/live-layer.js`): every registration goes through an inner `cleanups` ledger (`listen()` wraps `addEventListener`/`removeEventListener`, timers pair with `clearTimeout`/`clearInterval`) and the return value is a one-shot uninstaller; `liveDiagUninstall` only remembers "where the previous install went", so a repeated `apply` tears the old one down before installing the new one (no stacking). `src/client.js`'s `apply` gains a "2e." block: `ctx.effect(() => installLiveDiagnostics() || undefined)` and `ctx.effect(() => installLiveBootRestore() || undefined)`; both new names join `src/live-layer.js`'s export table (`exportListHonest` stays green).
  ③ **Zero semantic change**: log wording, the 60s beat, the `once` semantics and the boot trace are kept word for word — only the registration site moves from module scope into the fiber.
  **Why**: the official plugin contract says so verbatim (`dsh-agent-preset/skills/cordis-plugin-development/references/ui-plugin.md`): "Keep factories free of side effects. Register styles, timers, listeners and other resources inside `apply` with `ctx.effect`/`ctx.on` and return their cleanup functions." The causal link between this leak and the composer focus loss is not yet proven, but the leak itself is a proven resource leak (one extra pair of window listeners plus one extra 60s timer per instance, and one pixel request per diagnostic line) — at minimum an amplifier that gets noisier the longer the page lives, and a direct violation of the host contract.
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` ⇒ **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (480)`** (476 → 480) — a new `liveFlagChecks.topLevelText()` (**it digs out function bodies only, never plain blocks**) plus two structural assertions: `moduleScopeQuiet` (no `addEventListener`/`setInterval`/`setTimeout` call may appear at `src/live-layer.js`'s module scope) and `installersWired` (`src/client.js` must wire both through `ctx.effect(() => install…() || undefined)`), each with a negative control: a synthetic "historical shape" (three module-scope side effects) must go red while the installed-in-a-function shape must pass, and "calling the installer bare" and "wiring only half" must both be rejected. ⚠️ The first version of the guard judged "module scope" by brace-balancing depth, so a call inside a top-level `try { setTimeout(…) } catch {}` sat at depth 1 and was read as "not top level" ⇒ **vacuously green (a missed catch)**; the function bodies must be cut out first. **Independent proof of teeth (mutants, `.test-cache/mutate-diag.mjs`)**: A moves the 60s beat back to module scope ⇒ the main assertion and its negative control both go red (`moduleScopeCalls=1`); B replaces the `ctx.effect` wiring with a bare call ⇒ the wiring assertion and its negative control go red (`src=false`); both restored and everything is green again. ⚠️ The script's restore used to work by **substring** replacement, and `let liveDiagUninstall = null;` is exactly a prefix of the mutated line ⇒ the restore wrote the mutation back into the file (complete with a literal `{NL}`); it now works on **whole trimmed lines** and ships a `status` self-check. `lib/client.js` rebuilt (same commit as `src/**`, `test/verify-client-sync.mjs` ⇒ `CLIENT SYNC CHECKS PASSED`): in the bundle `function installLiveDiagnostics()` sits at `lib/client.js:17628`, `function installLiveBootRestore()` at `:18785`, and the wiring at `:26009-26010`. The full chain `build` / `verify` / `verify:docs` / `smoke` / `git diff --check` all exit 0. **Client-side only ⇒ a page refresh is enough.**

- **Fix (same class, second site): the "defer hidden-period rotation until visible" listeners were also at module scope — moved into `ctx.effect` alongside the entry above, and the guard now covers every `src/**/*.js`** (same source: a proven defect surfaced while investigating the upstream issue "while a wallpaper plays the composer loses focus every 0.9–3.6 s, and pausing immediately restores typing"; **no** factory switch changed, **no** stored dimension changed, `settingsVersion` **not** bumped).
  **What was done**: ① the original shape at `src/client.js:1355-1362` registered `visibilitychange` on `document` and `focus` on `window` (a fallback; the comment says verbatim that a missing event will not lose the pending work) inside a **module-scope** `try{}catch{}`, with `resumePendingRotation()` as the callback — every revision reload stacked another pair and never removed it. ② extracted `installRotationResumeListeners()`: both registrations are tracked in an inner `bound` ledger and the return value is a one-shot uninstaller (one `removeEventListener` per entry); `apply`'s "2e." block gains a third `ctx.effect(() => installRotationResumeListeners() || undefined)` (that block's comment now names both sites). ③ Semantics unchanged word for word: the same `resumePendingRotation`, the same two events, the same fallback note.
  **Why**: with the first fix in place, a module-scope sweep over all `src/**/*.js` (38 files, recursive; `.test-cache/scan-toplevel.mjs`) found exactly these two `addEventListener` calls left at module scope — same shape, same cause, same consequence: each module-body re-run adds another pair, so one `visibilitychange`/`focus` runs N times (this session measured 214 page ids in a single document). Fixing one site without spreading the guard over the whole tree would let the same style grow back.
  **Guards**: `test/verify-scene-live.mjs` ⇒ **`ALL SCENE-LIVE CHECKS PASSED (483)`** (480 → 483) — a new assertion "no `src/**/*.js` module registers timers or listeners at module scope" (`liveFlagChecks.srcTreeOffenders()` walks the whole tree and returns the offending file list; only an empty array passes); `installersWired` tightened from two wires to **three** (missing `installRotationResumeListeners` ⇒ "hidden, then visible again" no longer resumes the deferred rotation); two new negative controls: `src/client.js`'s historical shape (that listener pair at module scope) must go red, and wiring only two of the three installers must be rejected. **Independent proof of teeth (mutants, `.test-cache/mutate-diag.mjs` extended to A/B/C/D)**: C moves the pair back to module scope and D removes the third wire; with all four applied **7 assertions go red** (including the tree-wide assertion and both new negative controls, `treeOffenders=2`: `src/client.js` plus the A-mutated `src/live-layer.js`), and after `restore` all 483 are green with `git diff --check` at exit 0. `lib/client.js` rebuilt (same commit as `src/**`, `test/verify-client-sync.mjs` ⇒ `CLIENT SYNC CHECKS PASSED`). **Client-only change ⇒ a page refresh is enough.**

- **Fix: on a not-yet-restarted backend the update-notice art probe turned into a flamethrower — the probe now caches its verdict for the whole page load and backs off exponentially (at most 8 requests in 90 s, down from 61)** (source: the F12 console the user pasted from the desktop client — roughly 150 red `about-qr/update-notice.jpg` 404 lines; `test/verify-about.mjs` 78 → 87 checks).
  **Symptom**: every panel mount started a fixed 1.5 s cadence that kept firing `HEAD` requests for the full 90 s wait = 60 requests; opening the panel a few times overlaid the "About" panel and the notice mounts and flushed 150+ 404 lines into the console. (The 404 itself is the known window on this machine — the route whitelist is read once when the DSH process starts, see the entry above; a restart makes it 200. The requests carry `no-store` / `no-cache`, so this was never a caching problem — it is a **noise** problem.)
